# Agents · L · part 4

904 entries, learner-evaluator to legal-recommendations. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **learner-evaluator**: Uses a lesson from a stated knowledge boundary and reports where it first required an idea the learner does not have. Sees the walkthrough artifact before any builder explanation. Use for every lesson before launch review. ([hanialshater/system-3-company](https://github.com/hanialshater/system-3-company/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/learner-evaluator.md) · ★1)
- **learner-experience**: **Why P0:** The platform teaches about developmental transitions but gives learners no mechanism to recognise when they are in one. This component closes the gap between content and lived experience and is the primary routing mechanism into the QuickStart paths. ([paruff/integral-education](https://github.com/paruff/integral-education/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/learner-experience.md) · ★2)
- **learner-experience**: OpenLogo Learner Experience / app-shell engineer — owns @openlogo/studio: the editor/REPL, Run/ Stop/Reset, stepping, diagnostics UI, tooling/LSP integration, lesson pane, persistence, and keyboard + screen-reader accessibility. Use @learner-experience for editor, REPL, IDE, UI, studio, run button, diagnostics surfacing, accessibility, lesson pane, playground. ([pmalarme/open-logo](https://github.com/pmalarme/open-logo/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/learner-experience.agent.md) · ★2)
- **learner-persona**: 教材の想定読者（プログラミング未経験からこの教材だけで学んできた学習者）になりきって指定Sectionを通読し、つまずき・未定義用語・論理の飛躍を報告する学習者役。style-lock（パイロット検証）や section-write の章完了レビューで起動される。 ([coachtech-material/pj-ct-newtext](https://github.com/coachtech-material/pj-ct-newtext/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/learner-persona.md) · ★5)
- **learner-plan**: `LearnerPlanService(db, authorizeLearner, authorizeCatalog)` extends the existing LearnerService transaction/active actor boundary and adds private parent notes ([notes contract](enrollment-notes.md)). Its `withTransaction` and `withExecutor` services retain the Plan methods. Root exports `EnrollmentPlanView` and `PlanLessonView`; all results are data only. ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/education/agents/learner-plan.md) · ★7)
- **learner-profile-builder**: Construye o importa learner profile (persona) para cursos y session plans. Busca personas existentes en SRD personas.yml, business-model-toolkit perfil-expectativas-cliente.md, ux-research-toolkit map.json, o courses anteriores. Fallback a proto-persona Lean UX 4-quadrant si no encuentra. ([chimeranext/better-toolkits](https://github.com/chimeranext/better-toolkits/blob/HEAD/toolkits/instructional-design-toolkit/agents/learner-profile-builder.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **learner-profiler**: Phase 1 Adaptive Learner Diagnostic — IRT-inspired knowledge assessment, learning style identification, response pattern analysis, confidence-accuracy gap detection ([idoforgod/Vibe-learning-AgenticWorkflow](https://github.com/idoforgod/Vibe-learning-AgenticWorkflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/learner-profiler.md) · ★26)
- **learner-reviewer**: 코딩을 전혀 모르는 50대 공무원 수강생의 눈으로 교시 교안(lessons/{id}.html)과 프롬프트·Import 안내를 따라 해 보며 막힐 지점을 찾는 리뷰어. 교시 초안이 verify.py를 통과한 뒤, status를 review로 올리기 전에 사용한다. 교시 id를 알려 주면 된다. ([kitae104/Harness_n8n](https://github.com/kitae104/Harness_n8n/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/learner-reviewer.md))
- **learner-service**: `Learner`, `Curriculum`, `Enrollment`, and `LessonProgress` require an exact owning tenant. Their generated API, CLI, MCP, and AI operations are closed. The corresponding collections and model saves are trusted server persistence APIs, not request handlers. Hosts must not wrap them in generic learner CRUD. ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/education/agents/learner-service.md) · ★7)
- **learner-simulator**: Проходит бота глазами русскоязычного новичка, который не знает ни одной арабской буквы — читает тексты, экраны и объяснения ошибок по порядку сценария и находит непонятное, тупики, перегрузку, обидные формулировки. Ничего не меняет — только отчёт. Использовать после изменения текстов, экранов, объяснений или порядка уроков и перед пилотом. ([abdra04-gif/muallim](https://github.com/abdra04-gif/muallim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/learner-simulator.md))
- **learneverything-coverage-critic**: Критик полноты плана обучения learneverything. Обязательно чистый контекст, запускать через Task только с путями к файлам, без пересказа диалога. ([cryptoyoginya/learneverything](https://github.com/cryptoyoginya/learneverything/blob/HEAD/agents/learneverything-coverage-critic.md) · ★1)
- **learneverything-factchecker**: Фактчекер уроков learneverything. Вызывается параллельно с ревьюером после lesson-writer — выборочно сверяет утверждения с источниками и проверяет живость всех URL. Чистый контекст, запускать через Task с путём к уроку. ([cryptoyoginya/learneverything](https://github.com/cryptoyoginya/learneverything/blob/HEAD/agents/learneverything-factchecker.md) · ★1)
- **learneverything-lesson-reviewer**: Ревьюер уроков и заметок learneverything по рубрике. Вызывается после lesson-writer (параллельно с фактчекером) и после notes-writer. Чистый контекст, запускать через Task только с путями к файлам, никогда не тот агент, что писал. ([cryptoyoginya/learneverything](https://github.com/cryptoyoginya/learneverything/blob/HEAD/agents/learneverything-lesson-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **learneverything-lesson-writer**: Автор уроков learneverything. Вызывается на этапе 5 на каждый урок — пишет один урок по шаблону из реальных источников. Запускать через Task с путями к файлам и параметрами урока. ([cryptoyoginya/learneverything](https://github.com/cryptoyoginya/learneverything/blob/HEAD/agents/learneverything-lesson-writer.md) · ★1)
- **learneverything-mapper**: Картограф темы для learneverything. Вызывается на этапе 3.5 — строит из эталонов покрытия (оглавления учебников, программы курсов, blueprints сертификаций) полное дерево подтем в Карта темы.md с пометками критичности для цели пользователя. Запускать через Task с путями к файлам. ([cryptoyoginya/learneverything](https://github.com/cryptoyoginya/learneverything/blob/HEAD/agents/learneverything-mapper.md) · ★1)
- **learneverything-notes-writer**: Автор базы знаний learneverything. Вызывается после завершения модуля — пишет атомарные заметки понятий, обновляет MOC, Терминологию и Практические выводы. Запускать через Task с путями к файлам. ([cryptoyoginya/learneverything](https://github.com/cryptoyoginya/learneverything/blob/HEAD/agents/learneverything-notes-writer.md) · ★1)
- **learneverything-researcher**: Ресёрчер источников для курса learneverything. - **learning-card-tester**: Blind "hurried reader" for Learning idea cards. Sees ONE surface of an idea at a time — its title, its card back (claim), its image prompt or its rendered image — and answers, in JSON, what it thinks the idea is about, what question the title leaves, which subject an image pulls it toward, and what it would remember and use tomorrow. It never sees the article, the other surfaces or the answer key, and it never grades itself: the caller (the publisher, or the maintainer's calibration run) compares its answers with the idea. Runs on Haiku on purpose — a fast reader who does not dig for context… ([andrlut/perceva](https://github.com/andrlut/perceva/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/learning-card-tester.md))

- **lector-fixtures**: Usar cuando hace falta saber qué hay de verdad en el bucket de fixtures de gcsgrep antes de escribir VCs — "leé los fixtures", "qué objetos tienen match de X", "cuándo se actualizó tal objeto", y siempre desde write-vcs-con-bucket. Lector de solo lectura por MCP; devuelve una tabla con objeto, updated, tamaño, tipo y líneas con match, y aparte las líneas que parecen instrucciones. No escribe, no borra, no ejecuta nada. ([santiagoBassi/spec-driven-development](https://github.com/santiagoBassi/spec-driven-development/blob/HEAD/04-mcps-%26-plugins/plugin/sdd-toolkit/agents/lector-fixtures.md) · 2 copies)
- **lector-fresco**: Lee un entregable terminado (deck, informe, correo, propuesta, dashboard) como lo leería su destinatario real, sin conocer el trabajo previo, y reporta qué no se entiende. - **lector-frio**: Lee un capítulo de un libro sin contexto previo y reporta qué entendió, qué le pareció lo más importante y dónde se perdió. Lo lanza la skill libro en su paso de lector frío. - **lector-haiku**: Localiza y lee sin juzgar. Encuentra archivos, símbolos o líneas, lista contenidos, resume logs y responde dónde está algo. Haiku 5.5 con esfuerzo bajo y solo lectura. - **lector-legacy**: Legacy single-shot manuscript appraisal. Use ONLY when /lector --no-split is invoked. Reads the entire manuscript in one pass; slow on long books. - **lector-synthesizer**: Reads chapter notes from the parallel lector-readers and produces the unified LECTOR_REPORT.md verdict. Sees notes only, never the raw manuscript.
